How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Events/Calendar Events Table
|Icon|Event|Schedule|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Jacob's Farming Contest]]|[[Jacob's Farming Contest]]|Every 3 days, lasts from XX:15 to XX:35 in real life's time.|Allows players to compete in 3 randomly chosen crops. Participants are awarded Jacob's Tickets ([[Jacob's Ticket]]) and [[Medals]].|
|[[Starlyn Contest]]|Starlyn Contests ([[Starlyn Contest]])|Every day in-game.|Allows players to complete various tasks on [[Galatea]] islands to achieve certain brackets. Participants are awarded Agatha's Coupons ([[Agatha's Coupon]]) and other rewards.|
|[[Dark Auction]]|[[Dark Auction]]|Every 3 days, starts at XX:55 in real life's time.|Allows players to bid against each other for unique items.|

# Events/Mayor Events Table
|Icon|Event|Mayor|Time|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mythological Ritual]]|[[Mythological Ritual]]|[[Diana]]|During the entire mayor's tenure.|Dig out [[Griffin Burrows]] and spawn Mythological Creatures ([[Mythological]]).|
|[[Fishing Festival]]|[[Fishing Festival]]|[[Marina]]|1st to 3rd of each SkyBlock Month.|Fish up Sharks ([[Sharks (disambiguation)]]).|
|[[Fishing Festival]]|[[Fishing Festival]]|[[Foxy]]|22 of Late Summer to 24 of Late Summer.|Fish up Sharks ([[Sharks (disambiguation)]]).|
|[[Mining Fiesta]]|[[Mining Fiesta]]|[[Cole]]|During the entire mayor's tenure.|Get +25 [[Mining Wisdom]], +25% [[Mining Fortune]], and obtain Refined Minerals ([[Refined Mineral]]) and Glossy Gemstones ([[Glossy Gemstone]]).|
|[[Mining Fiesta]]|[[Mining Fiesta]]|[[Foxy]]|22 of Late Summer to 28 of Late Summer.|(same as above)|
|[[Carnival]]|[[Carnival]]|[[Foxy]]|During the entire mayor's tenure|Play minigames to earn [[Carnival Tokens]], allowing you to purchase [[Carnival Masks]] and Event Perks.|
|[[Richard]]|Stock Exchange ([[Stock of Stonks]])|[[Diaz]]|From 27 of Late Spring to 31 of Late Spring, then repeating every SkyBlock Month for their entire duration, until the end of the mayor’s tenure|Participate in the Stonks Auction for a chance to win [[Stock of Stonks]]! Trade them for extravagant items at the [[Trade Center]].|

# Events/Yearly Events System Table
|Icon|Event|Description|
|-|-|-|
|[[Year of the Pig]]|[[Year of the Pig]]|Celebrate the Year of the Pig by chasing Shiny Pigs and collecting their treasures to unlock exclusive rewards!|
|[[Year of the Seal]]|[[Year of the Seal]]|The Year of the Seal event primarily consists of [[Fishing]], playing the Beach Ball Minigame, and spending [[Fishy Treat]] at [[Lukas the Aquarist]].|
|[[Year of the Witch]]|[[Year of the Witch]]|The Year of the Witch event primarily consists of brewing Witches Stews ([[Year of the Witch]]), collecting special ingredients ([[Year of the Witch]]) from mobs, and spending Ingredients ([[Year of the Witch]]) at [[Agnes]] and [[Alcina]].|

# Events/Yearly Events Table
|Icon|Event|Duration|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hoppity]]|[[Hoppity's Hunt]]|Early Spring 1 - Late Spring 31|Help [[Hoppity]] find his [[Chocolate Rabbits]] across SkyBlock.|
|[[Mayor Election]]|Election Over ([[Mayor Election]])|Late Spring 27|The Mayor is selected until the next election ends!|
|[[Traveling Zoo]]|[[Traveling Zoo]]|Early Summer 1-3 Early Winter 1-3|Oringo the Traveling Zookeper ([[Oringo]]) is visiting SkyBlock with pets to trade!|
|[[Mayor Election]]|Election Booth Opens ([[Mayor Election]])|Late Summer 27|The Mayor election booth opens in the community center!|
|[[Harvest Feast]]|[[Harvest Feast]]|Early Autumn 1 - Late Autumn 31|4 crops will be in-season and have a chance to drop [[Seasoning]] and [[Rare Crops]]. Donate [[Seasoning]] to the [[Communal Stew]] and be rewarded with Feast Flasks ([[Feast Flask]])!|
|[[Spooky Festival]]|[[Spooky Festival]]|Autumn 29-31|Autumn is in full swing and the air is full of fright. Mob drops have a chance to contain Candy ([[Candy (disambiguation)]]), which can be traded with the [[Fear Mongerer]] for rare items!|
|[[Season of Jerry]]|[[Season of Jerry]]|Late Winter 24-26|The Jerrys are hard at work trying to craft enough Gifts for all of SkyBlock, but an army of enemies are on the attack! Help protect [[Jerry's Workshop]] so that everyone can go home with Gifts!|
|[[New Year Celebration]]|[[New Year Celebration]]|Late Winter 29-31|To celebrate the SkyBlock New Year, the [[Baker]] is giving out free Cake ([[New Year Cake]]).|

# Fairy Souls/List/Dungeons
Note: Click on the rows to keep track of the Fairy Souls collected. This persists even after reloading or refreshing the page.:
|No.|Room Name|Area|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|
|D-1|Diagonal ([[Catacombs Rooms]])|In a 1*3 room with zigzag-shaped passages, many boxes made of wooden stair blocks, and a knight statue made of diorite, there will be 2 Torches on a wall and move to the nearest room on the left. Go behind the Dark Oak box and the Fairy Soul will be in a Pond of 2*2 blocks.|✔|
|D-2|Tomioka ([[Catacombs Rooms]])|In the 1*1 room where [[Tomioka]] is at, go to the second Jail Cell on the left, and walk through the secret Entrance to Tomioka. Underneath the Staircase, there will be the Fairy Soul.|✔|
|D-3|Raccoon ([[Catacombs Rooms]])|In a 1*1 room with a chest on top of a broken cubic prison, teleport to the Vines near where some skulls lay, and then you will find the Fairy Soul behind some Leaves.|✕|
|D-4|Crypt ([[Catacombs Rooms]])|REMOVED SINCE UNKNOWN VERSION DUE TO DUPLICATED ID; In a 1*2 room with 2 staircases and 5 stone coffins in the center, go to some sticks and go to the top. The Fairy Soul will be on the Mossy Stone Bricks.|✕|
|D-5|Stairs ([[Catacombs Rooms]])|In a 2*2 room with a big staircase on the sides, go to a little pocket next to where the stairs lead. The Fairy Soul will be near some Mossy Stone Bricks.|✔|
|D-6|Leaves ([[Catacombs Rooms]])|In a prison-like 1*1 room, there will be 2 cells next to each other and on top will be an empty area. The Fairy Soul is in the top-left corner on top of the cells.|✕|
|D-7|Mossy ([[Catacombs Rooms]])|In a 1*4 room with a chest hanging from the ceiling, beneath that is a hole with some Vines made out of Leaves. Behind those Vines, there will be a Fairy Soul there.; This one has the same ID of another one.|✔|
|D-8|Super-Tall ([[Catacombs Rooms]])|In a 2*2 room with a huge wooden gate (if the lever isn't pulled), a lever on a polished granite block on the top of a broken pillar, and a bunch of Withermancers, go to a large space with few stone bricks. The Fairy Soul is found behind some Leaves on the wall on the opposite side of the staircase.|✕|
|D-9|Admin ([[Catacombs Rooms]])|In the 1*1 Admin room (with coffins of the admins), the Fairy Soul is found behind Simon Hypixel's coffin.|✕|
Linked from: [[Fairy Souls]], [[Fairy Souls/List]]
